# Exact catalogue of all 350 fixed batches

Generated from the unchanged batch manifests and the latest saved first-ten batch register. No batch was executed to prepare this catalogue. Counts are work items and overlap across workstreams.

Use this catalogue with `01_LOCAL_IMPLEMENTATION_RUNBOOK.md`. Each entry specifies exact membership, its saved status, the relevant complete workstream procedure, entry gate and completion evidence. The row order below is the original register order, not alphabetical file order.

## Per-batch instructions

### 1. S01: Checkpoint controls

**Procedure:** runbook section 7, plus sections 4-6 for state, inventory and common batch controls.
**Membership:** `plan/batches/S01.csv`. Exactly **1 control task** in this immutable scope.
**Scope SHA-256:** `18676a3a29d1f9171eb9dc82033df62e6f319ff8b06a1eeb8c3a83c4a1f1dce1`.
**Saved status:** Complete. Historical original plan status: Planned.

**Required work:** Load source registers; protect all designated keepers; exclude absent targets and completed rename operations; create an append-only run ledger.

**Start gate:** Before any execution.

**Completion evidence:** Saved source checksums, keeper set, 54 absent-target exclusions and 203 completed-rename exclusions. Preserve all 67 absence dispositions and their evidence levels.

**Mutation boundary:** this fixed review/control batch does not authorize Dropbox writes. Create applicable R/X/C/Q/G children only after the complete preflight and exact approval. V/T children have their own versioned memberships.

**Last saved attempt result:** Locked dated source checksums, protected keepers/replacements and operation exclusions.

**Outstanding item / next action:** Controls are checkpoint-based; refresh affected records before any future mutation.

### 2. S02: One BMP and one JPG

## Dynamic child batches

Use the full procedures in runbook sections 17-24. These are created from validated applicable subsets; their counts cannot be predetermined from the old review totals.

| Prefix | Maximum | Role |
|---|---:|---|
| R | 25 files | Filename-only moves and independent verification |
| X | 25 targets | Exact duplicate deletion and retained-keeper verification |
| C | 5 sources | Real BMP decoding, JPEG encoding, upload, re-download and QA; source retained |
| Q | 5 sources | Separately approved verified BMP source retirement |
| G | 1 folder | Folder move with complete subtree verification/rebasing |
| V | 25 retained assets | Actual image/video identity and in-situ evidence review |
| T | 100 retained IDs | Family-level close-out and lineage verification |
| Z01 | Paginated | Fresh whole-library closing audit |

New files, restored IDs, new duplicate candidates and additional identity issues require explicitly versioned supplemental scopes. Do not silently insert them into the fixed 350 batches.
